# Review — 052-spec-supersession-and-consolidation

## Summary

Five passes over 052's window since its reopening (diff-base cb30b333, base-source reopen) to HEAD, by one agent that read the nine loaded rule files whole. The window holds e3848348 (052 task 4, retire-feature's name screen under allow-sequential), which no review had read and which this one reviewed in depth, and the commits ced8c7ca, edd08387, ea859f7c and 3297fbd6, whose code 048's review (2f79a9ee to ced8c7ca, with its re-read rounds) and 051's 2026-10-29 review read in depth; per AGENTS.md's rule on a window several specs share, this review read their hunks only where they edited 052's artifacts or the deliverable it reads whole.

Coverage: examined counts 8 of the 30 scope entries, each read whole: 052's spec, plan, tasks and content-move scenario (its stranded-session scenario read whole too, as contract), retire_feature.rs, framework/commands/consolidate.md and framework/reference/consolidate.md, and runtime/tests/consolidate_hook.rs. unread names the other 22: the generated command and reference mirrors and the fold, help and target sources the two earlier reviews read; descriptions.rs, CHANGELOG.md and rewrite_spec_links.rs, read in part (e3848348's hunks, and rewrite_spec_links.rs's module doc, skipped reasons and walk); and scripts/mutation/retire-feature-screens.py, which this run's fixes added and the fix-round re-read reads.

Fixed in the run:

- BE-INPUT-002 (MUST): retire-feature --allow-sequential accepted a name holding a path separator, and on a fixture `--feature 012-src/scenarios` deleted the spec's scenarios. Both arguments now pass validate_session_feature, the screen rewrite-spec-links applies to its `to`; a_name_holding_a_path_separator_is_refused_with_allow_sequential holds it, and M1 of retire-feature-screens.py shows it failing.
- A broken contract: consolidating a spec into itself removed it, the spec.md guard satisfied by the directory being removed. retire-feature refuses a feature that is its own fold target, and consolidate's step 1 says so, naming no primitive in backticks, since the walker dispatches every backticked one in a step; a_feature_that_is_its_own_fold_target_is_refused and M2 hold it.
- The changelog bullet for e3848348 carries its (spec 052) tag, and retire_feature::run's # Errors names both new refusals.

Waived by the rule: consolidate_hook.rs hand-rolls the command's steps 4 and 5, so it proves the documented order through the shipped hook rather than the command's walk; each case still fails when the order or the 051 re-point fix is removed.

Six stored decisions expired, each checked against its file first: the dot-fragment cut, the unjudged .githooks destination, fold's step-11 resumption row, the unlistable directory the walk dropped and the kept-tails prefix no longer stand in the code, and the legacy-constitutions-region migration they name is gone. 26 are retained, on files this run did not read.

2 fix round(s): 2 re-read, 0 diff-checked, 0 exempt, 0 not re-read.

## Observations

- bug — consolidating a spec into itself (--into naming the source) removes the spec: retire-feature's anti-stranding guard is satisfied by the very directory being removed, and nothing in consolidate.md or rewrite-spec-links refuses source == target — `runtime/src/primitives/retire_feature.rs:106-120` (breaks the spec's contract) — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — the changelog bullet for e3848348 omits the `(spec 052)` tag its neighbours carry; the claim it makes ('a `feature` that `parse_feature_dir` does not accept is refused') is true, but see the BE-INPUT-002 finding: parse_feature_dir accepts names with a separator — `runtime/CHANGELOG.md:1083` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — consolidate_hook.rs hand-rolls steps 4 and 5 (consolidate() calls rewrite-spec-links and retire-feature directly, then `git add -A specs`), so it proves the documented order through the shipped hook but not the command's own walk, step 6 or the report — `runtime/tests/consolidate_hook.rs:128-146` — **waived**: The command is a markdown walk the runtime cannot drive; the scenario's contract is the hook's behavior on the order, which the test establishes with a real git commit, and both cases fail if the 051 re-point fix or the order is removed. Fixture-only `git add -A` is not the repository-tree rule. (decided by the rule)
- convention — the `run` doc comment's # Errors paragraph still says InvalidArgument is returned when `feature` is not branch-scoped and `allow-sequential` was not set; since e3848348 it is also returned for a name parse_feature_dir rejects when the flag is set — `runtime/src/primitives/retire_feature.rs:45-52` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
